Kids' Events Digest — Tue Oct 20 → Mon Oct 26, 2026 (ages under 7)

About this digest
  • Next update: Sat Oct 24 by 9 PM PT (every 5 days, delivered to this chat).
  • Window: Tue Oct 20, 2026 through Mon Oct 26, 2026 — 7 days, America/Los_Angeles. Swept Mon Oct 19, 2026 (PT), with a live-browser verification pass the same day.
  • Home base: ZIP 94560 (Newark, CA). Distances: est. driving miles from 94560, taken from this run's sweep parts.
  • Coverage: baby/toddler/preschool storytimes (ages 0–6) plus all-ages kids/family events. 548 library events in the day lists below (Tue 10/20: 120 · Wed 10/21: 122 · Thu 10/22: 108 · Fri 10/23: 40 · Sat 10/24: 78 · Sun 10/25: 23 · Mon 10/26: 66) plus 89 Beyond listings (65 dated day-picks + 24 multi-day/standing lines, counted once); 637 total listings. School-age-only, teen, adult, reading-to-dog, and canceled events excluded.
  • Verification: V = confirmed live this run (Mon 10/19) — text sweep + a live-browser pass that pinned 207 events across 15 sources (Berkeley, Livermore, Santa Clara City, Sunnyvale, Redwood City, South San Francisco, San Mateo City, Mountain View, San Bruno, Daly City, Menlo Park, San Leandro, SFPL's 86 dated instances, Santa Cruz Downtown/La Selva Beach, Children's Fairyland). U = pattern/carryover — not live-verified this run. Library totals: 534 V, 14 U. Beyond: 63 V, 28 U. Source-count reconciliation: ACL, Oakland, Hayward, Pleasanton, SJPL, SCCLD, Palo Alto, CCCL and SCPL reconciled cleanly (calendar total − excluded = in-scope, per day). Named discrepancies: El Cerrito facet lists 2 events but the branch listing renders only 1 (extra is likely a stale index entry — no storytime carried); Concord facet lists 13, 12 render (the 12 reconcile exactly); Dougherty Station "All Ages, All Moves" appears twice as separate records — counted once. Count-unknown systems (Berkeley, Livermore, San Leandro, RWC, SSF, Sunnyvale, Santa Clara City, Mountain View, SFPL) were re-pinned by the browser pass and are now V; Sunnyvale remains partially verifiable (LibCal calendar 404s — 1 event V, rest U). Tap the V or U to open the event's page.
  • Flags: "free ticket" = pick up at the Children's desk before the program (Hayward rule — see Closures: free ticket 30 min before, every adult AND child, no advance reservations). ⚠️ = choking-hazard flags for a 2-year-old (LEGO/small-piece/bead crafts). "borderline" = skews school-age but all-ages. All-day events are in Activities with "all day" wording so the renderer buckets them separately.
  • All library events are free unless noted. Non-library sources (bookstores, parks, museums, zoos, nature, art studios, Eventbrite, parent calendars) are mostly PAID — costs shown distinctly (PAID/FREE) in Beyond.
  • Layout: each day is split into Storytimes vs Activities (nearest-first within each). A Nearest | Earliest | Latest toggle re-sorts every list; all-day events go in the All-day bucket.
